CORPORATE PROFILE                                                   公司簡介
SHOUGANG FUSHAN RESOURCES GROUP LIMITED                             首鋼福山資源集團有限公司
is one of the most sizable integrated coking coal corporations in   為中國中西部 最具規模之綜合焦煤企業之一。
CENTRAL-WESTERN CHINA.
Taking Shanxi Province as its major investment base, it             以山西省作為主要投資基地，
is principally engaged in MINING OF COKING COAL,                    主要從事 焦煤開採、原焦煤及精焦煤生產及銷
PRODUCTION AND SALES OF RAW AND CLEAN COKING
                                                                    售業務。
COAL.
The Group has three premium operating coking coal mines and         本集團現有三座在產的優質焦煤礦及三座洗煤廠。
three coal preparation plants.

OPERATING MINES                                                        營運中之煤礦

XINGWU COAL MINE                                                       興無煤礦

•   6 km south of Liulin County and the mining right area extended     •   位於柳林縣以南6公里，採礦權區佔地約
    over 11.6 sq. km and spans 4.5 km east to west and 4.5 km              11.6平方公里，東西相距4.5公里，南北相距
    north to south                                                         4.5公里

•   Operation commenced in 1968                                        •   於一九六八年開始營運

•   Annual approved raw coking coal production capacity: 1.75          •   核准年原焦煤生產能力：175萬噸
    million tonnes

•   Operate a coal preparation plant with annual designed input        •   經營一座年入洗量為120萬噸之洗煤廠（於二
    processing capacity of 1.2 million tonnes (put into operation in       零零二年十月投產）
    October 2002)

•   Mainly production of hard coking coal                              •   主要生產硬焦煤

OPERATING MINES (continued)                                              營運中之煤礦（續）

COAL CHARACTERISTICS                                                     煤炭特徵

•     Located within the Lishi-Liulin mining area of Hedong coalfield,   •   位於河東煤田之離柳礦區，該區為中國優質
      one of China’s key reserve areas for high-quality hard coking          硬焦煤之主要儲量區域之一。
      coal.

•     Regarded as “panda coal” because of its scarcity and high          •   由於其稀有性和高經濟價值，被譽為「熊貓
      economic value.                                                        煤」。

•     The Group’s coking coal is of particularly good quality due to     •   本集團之焦煤發熱值高及焦結度強，加上灰
      its high calorific value and caking index but low ash and sulfur       份及硫份低，品質優越，使之非常適合用於
      content, characteristics that are highly desirable for coke and        生產焦炭及鋼鐵。
      steel making.

aforesaid financial risks and risk mitigation measures are elaborated       3「財務風險管理」中闡述。

the downstream steel industry and the supply of coking coal. During         應。於回顧年度內焦煤價格在前三季度下降，然
the year under review, coking coal prices have dropped in the first         後在第四季度緩慢地反彈。焦煤的市場整體價格
three quarters and then rebound slowly in the fourth quarter. The           在二零二零年按年下降。其中，二零二零年精焦
overall market prices of coking coal were dropped in 2020, of which,        煤平均市場價格按年下跌9%至14%。如煤價受
the average market prices of clean coking coal dropped by 9% to             壓，集團的業績將直接受到影響。
14% YoY in 2020. If coal price was dropping, the Group’s results

Operational risks include the estimation of remaining coal reserves,        營運風險包括評估剩餘的煤炭儲量、重續採礦權
the renewal of mining rights and the mining works. The lives of our         和採礦工作。煤礦的壽命主要基於評估剩餘的煤
coal mines are highly depended on the estimated remaining coal              炭儲量及重續採礦權的可能性。本集團煤炭儲量
reserves and the possibility to renew the mining rights. Engineering        之工程估計涉及工程師在編製該等資料時作出之
estimates of the Group’s coal reserves involved subjective judgements       主觀判斷，估算技術存在固有不精確性。如過往
by engineers that the inherent inaccuracy of technical estimation           之估算出現重大改變，煤礦的壽命或會縮短。另

THE GROUP

The Group’s principal business activities comprise coking coal mining,   本集團的主要業務為於中國從事焦煤炭開採、焦

the Group’s major subsidiaries in Liulin County, Shanxi Province, it     要附屬公司經營三座優質焦煤礦及其附屬的洗煤
operates three premium coking coal mines and their associated coal       廠，用以生產原焦煤及精焦煤。
preparation plants for the production of raw and clean coking coal.

approximately 3.23 million tonnes respectively, and the sales volume     分別約8萬噸和約326萬噸，錄得營業額約39.97

DIRECTORS’ BIOGRAPHIES                                                  董事簡介

Mr. Ding Rucai, aged 56, had acted as a deputy general manager          丁汝才先生， 56歲，自二零一四年八月擔任本
of the Company since August 2014, then was appointed as the vice        公司之副總經理一職，其後於二零一四年九月獲

of the Company in September 2014 and was re-designated as the           經理，並由二零一八年一月二十九日起轉任為主
Chairman from 29 January 2018. Mr. Ding is also the chairman of         席。丁先生亦分別為執行委員會和提名委員會之
each of the Executive Committee and the Nomination Committee, a         主席、薪酬委員會之成員，及本集團若干附屬公
member of the Remuneration Committee, and a director of certain         司之董事。
subsidiaries of the Group.

Mr. Ding is a senior engineer in professor grade. He graduated          丁先生為教授級高級工程師。彼畢業於北京科技
from the School of Metallurgical and Ecological Engineering of the      大學冶金與生態工程學院，並取得鋼鐵冶金博士
University of Science and Technology Beijing with a doctoral degree     學位，彼亦曾在英國華威大學研修高級工商管
in ferrous metallurgy, he also studied senior business administration   理。丁先生於管理上市公司、收購合併、鋼鐵和
in the University of Warwick, United Kingdom. Mr. Ding has extensive    煤炭企業生產管理、項目工程建設、礦石和焦煤
experience in management of listed companies, mergers and               資源貿易、航運管理方面均具有豐富經驗。
acquisitions, production management of steel and coal industries,
project construction management, trading of iron ore and coking
coal resources and shipping management.

Previously, Mr. Ding was an executive director and the deputy           過往，丁先生曾擔任香港上市公司首長國際企業
managing director of Shougang Concord International Enterprises         有限公司（現稱首程控股）(SEHK:697)之執行董事
Company Limited (now known as Shoucheng Holdings) (SEHK:697)            及副董事總經理（由二零一四年九月至二零一八年

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                  企業管治報告

The Company is committed to maintaining good corporate                       本公司致力維持良好企業管治準則及程序，以維
governance standard and procedures to safeguard the interests of all         護全體股東利益，提高問責性及透明度。
shareholders and to enhance accountability and transparency.


COMPLIANCE WITH CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                         遵守企業管治守則
CODE

The Company has complied with the code provisions of the CG Code             本公司於回顧年度內已遵守企業管治守則的守則
during the year under review, save for the deviations from code              條文，惟以下守則條文第A.4.1條之偏離則除外︰
provision A.4.1 as follows:

Under code provision A.4.1, non-executive directors should be                根據守則條文第A.4.1條，非執行董事之委任應有
appointed for a specific term and subject to re-election.                    指定任期，並須接受重選。

None of the existing Non-executive Directors and Independent Non-            各現任非執行董事及獨立非執行董事概無特定任
executive Directors is appointed for a specific term, but according to       期，但根據細則，每位董事須至少每3年輪值退
the Articles, every Director is subject to retirement by rotation at least   任一次。
once every 3 years.

Since all Directors, including the Non-executive Directors and               由於所有董事（包括非執行董事及獨立非執行董
Independent Non-executive Directors, are also subject to retirement          事）均須根據細則於股東週年大會上輪值退任及由
by rotation and re-election by shareholders at annual general                股東重選，董事會因此認為此規定足以達至該條
meetings pursuant to the Articles, the Board therefore considers this        守則條文的相關目的。
requirement is sufficient to meet the underlying objective of the said
code provision.


MODEL CODE FOR SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS                                       進行證券交易的標準守則

Internal Control (continued)                                                內部監控（續）

Based on the defined objectives, the management of the Group                本集團管理層根據既定的目標，識別和評估潛在
identifies and evaluates significant risks, and subsequently selects,       的主要風險，並以此為基礎，選擇、制訂和實施
adopts and implements appropriate internal control procedures.              所需的內部監控措施，並通過持續監察的方式，
Through continuous monitoring, the management of the Group                  維繫有效的內部監控系統。本集團內部審計部對
maintains an effective internal control system. The internal audit          內部監控系統進行測試，並於發現監控缺陷時，
department of the Group conducts testing on the internal control            向管理層建議糾正措施，並於其後跟蹤糾正措施
system, reports deficiencies, recommends remedy solutions to the            的落實情況。
management and follows up implementation of recommendations.

The Audit Committee monitors, assesses and reviews the findings of          審核委員會就管理層及內部審計部對內部監控系
the internal control systems from the management and the internal           統的調查結果進行持續監督、評估和審議，並定
audit department of the Group on an ongoing basis, and regularly            期向董事會匯報審議結果。
report to the Board.

The Group sets up an internal audit department which directly reports       本集團設有內部審計部，直接向審核委員會匯
to the Audit Committee. The staffs of the internal audit department         報。內部審計部由具有專業資格和豐富經驗的成
comprised of qualified and experienced professional. Based on               員組成。根據以風險為導向的內部審計計劃，內
the risk-based internal audit plan, the internal audit department is        部審計部可以在不受限制的情況下，獨立地檢討
authorised unrestrictedly to conduct independent assessment on the          本集團所有業務、風險管理及內部監控系統。

The Group’s risk governance structure is based on a “Three Lines of         本集團以三道防線模式為基礎來支援董事會對風

to the Board on the effectiveness of the risk management system.            效。

functions and measures in each line is as follows:                          和措施如下：

risk appetite regarding the nature and magnitude and constructs a           接納的風險性質及程度，並建立了一個自上而
risk management procedures allowing a tone at the top and bottom            下，由下而上的風險管理程序。
up reporting.


Top-Down Approach                                                           自上而下

The Board, through the management of the Group, communicates to             董事會通過管理層對潛在影響本集團的固有風險
entities, divisions, operating units and subsidiaries regarding potential   和新生風險向相關主體、分部、業務單元或附屬
effects on inherent risks and emerging risks, and defines standards on      單位傳達，並就本集團的風險偏好和風險容忍度
the Group’s risk appetite and risk tolerance level.                         訂定可接納標準。

Bottom Up Approach                                                      由下而上

operating plan, the Group’s entities, divisions, operating units and    管理層傳達董事會的風險偏好，以及根據年度經

transferring and controlling, are used to manage the risks based on     會既定的風險偏好和風險容忍度，通過接受、迴
the assessment results and the risk appetite and risk tolerance level   避、轉移或控制的形式來應對風險。
defined by the Board.

and for reviewing its appropriateness and effectiveness. The Audit      責任對風險管理系統的合適性和有效性進行檢

review. The Group’s risk management system manages, but not             討。本集團的風險管理系統旨在管理而非消除未
eliminates, risks against the achievement of the Group’s objectives,    能達成本集團業務目標的風險，而且只能就不會
and provides a reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against          有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保
material misstatement or loss.                                          證。

AUDITOR                                                                 核數師

BDO Limited (“BDO”) resigned as the auditor of the Company with         香 港 立 信 德 豪 會 計 師 事 務 所 有 限 公 司（「立 信 德
effect from 1 August 2018 as the Board and the Audit Committee          豪」）已辭任本公司核數師，自二零一八年八月一
considered, in order to comply with good corporate governance           日起生效，原因為董事會及審核委員會考慮到立
practice and enhance the independence of the auditor, it was            信德豪已擔任本公司的核數師多年，本公司適合
appropriate for the Company to change the auditor as BDO had            更換核數師，以符合良好的公司管治常規，及提
been the auditor of the Company for a number of years. The Board,       升核數師的獨立性。按照審核委員會的建議，董
with the recommendation from the Audit Committee, resolved to           事會決議委任羅兵咸永道會計師事務所為本公司
appoint PricewaterhouseCoopers as the auditor of the Company with       的核數師，自二零一八年八月一日起生效，以填
effect from 1 August 2018 to fill the casual vacancy following the      補立信德豪辭任後之臨時空缺。
resignation of BDO.

Save as disclosed above, there is no other change of the Company’s      除上文所披露者外，本公司之核數師於過去3年
auditors in any of the preceding three years.                           內並無其他變動。

retire and, being eligible, offer themselves for reappointment at the   於應屆股東週年大會上退任，彼符合資格並願意
forthcoming annual general meeting. A resolution will be submitted      續聘。續聘羅兵咸永道會計師事務所為本公司核
to the AGM 2021 to reappoint PricewaterhouseCoopers as the              數師之決議案將於二零二一年股東週年大會上提

     2.2 Principles of consolidation and equity                              2.2 合併及權益會計原則
         accounting

          (i)   Subsidiaries                                                   (i)   附屬公司

                Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                  附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權之所
                entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                有實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集
                controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                  團從參與實體業務而承擔取得其可
                has rights to, variable returns from its involvement                 變回報之風險或享有可變回報之權
                with the entity and has the ability to affect those                  利，並有能力透過其對實體業務之
                returns through its power to direct the activities of                主導權影響該等回報時，則本集團
                the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the             控制該實體。附屬公司於控制權轉
                date on which control is transferred to the Group.                   移至本集團當日起全面綜合入賬，
                They are deconsolidated from the date that control                   並於終止控制權當日起終止綜合入
                ceases.                                                              賬。

                The acquisition method of accounting is used to                      本集團採用收購會計法將業務合併
                account for business combinations by the Group (refer                入賬（見附註2.3）。
                to note 2.3).

                Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                   集團內公司間交易、結餘及集團內
                gains on transactions between group companies are                    公司間交易未變現收益均會對銷。
                eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless             未變現虧損亦會對銷，惟該交易有
                the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                   證據顯示已轉讓資產出現減值則除
                of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                     外。附屬公司之會計政策已在有需
                subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                    要時作出更改，以確保與本集團所
                ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the                  採納有關政策一致。
                Group.

                Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of               附屬公司業績及權益中之非控股權
                subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                益分別於綜合損益及其他全面收益

         accounting (continued)

          (ii) Associates                                                         (ii) 聯營公司

               Associates are all entities over which the Group                       聯營公司指所有本集團對其有重大
               has significant influence but not control or joint                     影響力而無控制權或共同控制權之
               control. This is generally the case where the Group                    實體。通常是本集團擁有20%至
               holds between 20% and 50% of the voting rights.                        50%投票權之股權。聯營公司投
               Investments in associates are accounted for using                      資初步以成本確認，其後以權益會
               the equity method of accounting, after initially being                 計法入賬。
               recognised at cost.

               Under the equity method of accounting, the                             根據權益會計法，投資初步以成本
               investments are initially recognised at cost and                       確認，其後作出調整以於損益中確
               adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of                  認本集團應佔被投資方之收購後溢
               the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee                 利或虧損，及於其他全面收益內確
               in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of movements                  認本集團應佔被投資方之其他全面
               in other comprehensive income of the investee in                       收益變動。聯營公司及合營企業之
               other comprehensive income. Dividends received                         已收或應收股息被確認為投資賬面
               or receivable from associates and joint ventures are                   值之扣減。
               recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of
               the investment.

               When the Group’s share of losses in an equity-                         如果本集團應佔一項採用權益會計
               accounted investment equals or exceeds its interest in                 法計算之投資之虧損等同或超逾其
               the entity, including any other unsecured long-term                    於該實體之權益，包括任何其他無
               receivables, the Group does not recognise further                      抵押長期應收款項，則本集團並不
               losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made                     確認進一步之虧損，除非其代表其
               payments on behalf of the other entity.                                他實體負有責任或支付款項，則另
                                                                                      作別論。

               Unrealised gains on transactions between the Group                     本集團與其聯營公司及合營企業之
               and its associates and joint ventures are eliminated                   間之未變現交易收益會以本集團

          case of finished goods, comprises direct materials, direct             品，則包括直接物料、直接人工及適當
          labour and an appropriate proportion of overheads. Net                 比例之間接費用。可變現淨值為於日常
          realisable value represented the estimated net selling prices          業務過程中之估計銷售淨價格減作出銷
          in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs            售所需之估計成本。
          necessary to make the sale.

          contain significant financing components, when they                    財務元素則作別論。本集團持有應收貿

          cash flows and therefore measures them subsequently                    有關本集團之應收貿易賬項會計處理方

     2.20 Current and deferred income tax                                     2.20 即期及遞延所得稅

          The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax            期內所得稅開支或抵免為基於各司法權
          payable on the current period’s taxable income based on               區之適用所得稅率計算之即期應課稅收
          the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted         入（由暫時差額及未動用稅項虧損導致
          by changes in deferred income tax assets and liabilities              之遞延所得稅資產及負債變動調整）之
          attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax               應付稅項。
          losses.

          Current income tax                                                    即期所得稅

          The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of           即期所得稅支出根據本公司及其附屬公
          the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted at the end of           司及聯營公司經營及產生應課稅收入之
          the reporting period in the countries where the company               國家於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已頒佈
          and its subsidiaries and associates operate and generate              之稅務法例計算。管理層就適用稅務法
          taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                     例詮釋所規限之情況定期評估報稅表之
          positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in          狀況，並考慮稅務機關是否有可能接受
          which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation          不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根據最可能
          and considers whether it is probable that a taxation                  的金額或預期值來衡量其稅收餘額，具
          authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment. The                 體取決於哪種方法可以更好地預測不確
          Group measures its tax balances either based on the most              定性的解決方法。
          likely amount or the expected value, depending on which
          method provides a better prediction of the resolution of
          the uncertainty.

          Deferred income tax                                                   遞延所得稅

          Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability           遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產及負債之
          method, on temporary differences arising between the tax               稅基與其於綜合財務報表內之賬面值
          bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in          兩者之暫時差額悉數作出撥備。然而，
          the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred               倘遞延所得稅負債於商譽初步獲確認時
          income tax liabilities are not recognised if they arise from           產生，則遞延所得稅負債不予確認。倘
          the initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is            遞延所得稅乃因於業務合併以外交易中
          also not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of        初步確認資產或負債而產生，而於交易
          an asset or a liability in a transaction other than a business         時不會影響會計處理及應課稅損益，則
          combination that at the time of the transaction affects                遞延所得稅亦不予入賬。遞延所得稅採
          neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                用在報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅率
          income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that              （及稅法）及於有關遞延所得稅資產變現
          have been enacted or substantially enacted by the end of               或遞延所得稅負債結算時預期將會應用
          the reporting period and are expected to apply when the                之稅率（及稅法）釐定。
          related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred
          income tax liability is settled.

     2.23 Provisions                                                            2.23 撥備

          Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                   當本集團因過往事件須承擔現有法律或
          legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events,             推定責任，而解除責任將很可能需要資
          it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required             源流出，且有關金額已可作可靠估算時
          to settle the obligation and the amount can be reliably                  會確認撥備，惟不會就日後之經營虧損
          estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future                      確認任何撥備。
          operating losses.

          Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                     倘出現多項類似責任，是否需要資源流
          likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement                出以解除責任乃經考慮整體責任類別
          is determined by considering the class of obligations as a               後釐定。即使就同類責任中任何一項出
          whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an            現資源流出之可能性不大，仍會確認撥
          outflow with respect to any one item included in the same                備。
          class of obligations may be small.

          Sales of goods                                                            銷售貨品

          or service is transferred to the customer. Depending on                   時確認收益。貨品或服務之控制權是在
          the terms of the contract and the laws that apply to the                  一段時間內或於某一特定時點轉移，取
          contract, control of the good or service may be transferred               決於合約之條款及適用於合約之法律。
          over time or at a point in time.

          Control of the good or service is transferred over time if                貨品或服務之控制權是在一段時間內轉
          the Group’s performance:                                                  移，即本集團履約過程中：

          •    provides all of the benefits received and consumed                   •   提供客戶同時收到且消耗之所有利
               simultaneously by the customer;                                          益；

          •    creates or enhances an asset that the customer                       •   產生或增強由客戶控制之資產（當
               controls as the Group performs; or                                       本集團履約時）；或

          •    does not create an asset with an alternative use to                  •   不會產生對本集團具有可替代用途
               the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                      之資產，且本集團有強制執行權就
               payment for performance completed to date.                               累計至今已完成之履約部分收取款
                                                                                        項。

          If control of the goods or services transfers over time,                  倘貨品或服務之控制權在一段時間內轉
          revenue is recognised over the period of the contract by                  移，參照在整個合約期間已完成履約
          reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction                   責任之進度確認收益。否則，收益於客
          of that performance obligation. Otherwise, revenue is                     戶獲得貨品或服務控制權之特定時點確
          recognised at a point in time when the customer obtains                   認。
          control of the goods or service.

                      period.

                      Management considers that full provision would                           管理層認為，將就已知財務

                      past 3 years and adjusted to reflect current                             及前瞻性資料，包括影響客

                      the customers to settle the receivables.

          (c)   Liquidity risk                                                       (c)   流動資金風險

                Liquidity risk relates to the risk that the Group will                     流動資金風險與本集團無法履行其
                not be able to meet its obligations associated with its                    財務負債相關責任之風險有關。本
                financial liabilities. The Group is exposed to liquidity                   集團面對償付應付貿易賬項及其融
                risk in respect of settlement of trade payables and                        資責任以及其現金流量管理有關之
                its financing obligations, and also in respect of its                      流動資金風險。本集團之目標為將
                cash flow management. The Group’s objective is                             流動資產及資金承擔維持於合適水
                to maintain an appropriate level of liquid assets                          平，以應付其短期及長期流動資金
                and committed lines of funding to meet its liquidity                       需求。
                requirements in the short and long term.

                The Group manages its liquidity needs by carefully                         本集團於日常業務中謹慎監察潛在
                monitoring expected payments for potential                                 投資及現金流出之預期付款，以管
                investments as well as cash-outflows due in day-to-                        理其流動資金需要。本集團按日監
                day business. Liquidity needs are monitored on a day-                      察其流動資金需要。另每月釐定為
                to-day basis. Long-term liquidity needs for a 360-day                      期360日之長期流動資金需要。
                lookout periods are identified on a monthly basis.

     4.2 Impairment of goodwill                                              4.2 商譽減值

          forecast and the suitable discount rates in order to calculate       現金流量時，管理層會對未來收益及溢
          the present value. In the process of estimating expected             利作出假設。該等假設與未來事件及狀

          future revenues and profits. These assumptions relate                導致下一個財政年度商譽之賬面值須作
          to future events and circumstances. The actual results               出大幅調整。釐定合適之貼現率涉及估
          may vary and may cause a material adjustment to the                  計市場風險及資產特定風險因素之合適

          estimating the appropriate adjustment for market risk                礦權許可證剩餘期限介乎12至22年不
          and asset specific risk factors. In addition, since the cash         等，管理層認為，本集團能繼續以最低
          flow projections are extrapolated beyond 5 years and the             費用向有關當局重續採礦權許可證。減

          probable reserves of the coal mines using units of                   攤銷及折舊。本集團每年評估煤礦之估
          production method. The Group assesses on an annual                   計儲量。然而，本集團所持採礦權許可
          basis the estimated reserve of the coal mine. However, the           證剩餘期限介乎12至22年，較本集團

          the Group. Management is of the opinion that the Group               計有別，有關差異將影響估計出現變動

     To determine the recoverable amounts based on value-in-use             根據使用價值計算確定可收回金額，估計未
     calculations, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to        來現金流量採用可反映現時市場對金錢時間
     their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects        值及現金產生單位特定風險之評估之稅前貼

     specific to the CGUs.

     Cash flows were projected based on the financial budgets               現金流量乃根據管理層以編製之5年財務預
     prepared by management covering a 5-year period, the cash              算而預測，5年期間以後之現金流量使用下
     flows beyond the 5-year period are extrapolated using a steady         列穩定百分比增長率推算。
     percentage growth rate below.

     The remaining license period of the mining rights held by the          本集團持有之採礦權許可證剩餘期限介乎12

     management is of the opinion that the Group is able to renew           費用向有關當局重續採礦權許可證。
     the license of the mining rights from the relevant authority
     continuously and at minimal charges.

34. RESERVES                                                                34. 儲備

     (a) Statutory reserve                                                       (a) 法定儲備

          In accordance with the relevant laws and regulations of                    根據中國相關法律及法規，中國附屬公
          the PRC, the PRC subsidiaries are required to appropriate                  司須按照中國會計規例編製提撥10%
          10% of its profit after tax, prepared in accordance with the               之除稅後溢利至法定儲備基金，直至法
          accounting regulation in the PRC, to the statutory reserve                 定儲備結餘達註冊資本之50%為止。該
          fund until the statutory reserve balance reaches 50% of                    儲備可用作扣減任何虧損或撥充實繳資
          the registered capital. Such reserve may be used to reduce                 本。
          any losses incurred or for capitalisation as paid-up capital.


     (b) Other reserves                                                          (b) 其他儲備

          Pursuant to the relevant PRC regulations, the Group is                     根據相關中國法規，本集團須按照產量
          required to transfer production maintenance fee, safety                    等相關基準按固定利率將維簡費、安全
          fund and other expense of similar nature (“production                      費及類似性質的其他開支（
                                                                                                「維簡基金」）
          and maintenance funds”) at fixed rates based on relevant                   轉撥至專項儲備賬。維簡基金可於維簡
          bases, such as production volume, to a specific reserve                    及安全措施產生開支或資本開支時動
          account. The production and maintenance funds could                        用。已動用的維簡基金金額將從專項儲
          be utilised when expenses or capital expenditures on                       備賬轉撥至保留盈利。
          production maintenance and safety measures are incurred.
          The amount of production and maintenance funds utilised
          would be transferred from the specific reserve account to
          retained earnings.
